LS1 | Uniform Ax approximation and completed fixed fields | proved_under_exact_declared_background_and_supplied_data_hypotheses
Every algebraic element whose H-orbit differences have norm at most r is within 16r of an algebraic H-fixed element. Thus C^H is the closure of Omega^H, and C^(G_K)=K for finite K.

LS7 | Integral logarithms, unit quotient and cyclotomic period | proved_under_exact_declared_background_and_supplied_data_hypotheses
For every tilt element e with sharp one, the integral logarithm t_e equals ([e]-1) times a unit in Acris, including p=2. The chosen t has phi(t)=pt, Galois action chi(t), and is a BdR+ uniformizer.

LS9 | Frobenius injectivity through divided-power completion | proved_under_exact_declared_background_and_supplied_data_hypotheses
Witt Frobenius is injective on the completed divided-power ring and on its t-localization. The finite coefficient induction with explicit indices proves the required divisibility on A[w/p]; bounded truncations then prove completion injectivity.
